88. Bash, "Replace the paper mode graphs and run the figure script"

`````
mkdir -p /private/tmp/claude-502/-Users-axelfaes-workspace-ordo/e68b9e7f-9bfe-4218-be15-6dd18d5f22c0/scratchpad/fig && cat > /private/tmp/claude-502/-Users-axelfaes-workspace-ordo/e68b9e7f-9bfe-4218-be15-6dd18d5f22c0/scratchpad/fig/simple.py <<'EOF'
SEARCH_STOP = "search rounds: end at any 3 of 5 conditions, or after 4 rounds"
PATCHED = "Script: each patch applied, the compile, the reference checks"
WRITING = "/writing review, unless you said no; the draft writer applies what you pick"


def _search(after: str) -> tuple[tuple[Node, ...], tuple[Edge, ...]]:
    """The session's choice of one literature strategist or several with a merge."""
    nodes = (
        Node("S", STRUCTURE, "choice"),
        Node("L", "Literature strategist, unless you give your own sources", "maybe"),
        Node("T2", STARTS, "maybe"),
        Node("M", MERGE + "review.md and refs.bib", "maybe"),
        Node("LR", "You review the sources, optional", "stop"),
    )
    edges = (
        Edge("S", "L", ONE),
        Edge("S", "T2", SEVERAL),
        Edge("L", "L", SEARCH_STOP, "back"),
        Edge("T2", "T2", SEARCH_STOP, "back"),
        Edge("T2", "M"),
        Edge("L", "LR"),
        Edge("M", "LR"),
        Edge("LR", after),
    )
    return nodes, edges


def paper_full_graph() -> Graph:
    search_nodes, search_edges = _search("A")
    nodes = (
        Node("I", "Intake agent: you confirm", "stop"),
        *search_nodes,
        Node("A", "Structure architect"),
        Node("OA", "Outline approval", "stop"),
        Node("B", "Argument builder"),
        Node("W", "Draft writer, 2 calls: main.tex"),
        Node("V", "Visualization, with data: the figures placed by patch", "maybe"),
        Node("C", "Citation compliance: each claim against its source's full text"),
        Node("AB", "Abstract agent: a patch"),
        Node("P", "Peer reviewer, 2 calls"),
        Node("WR", "Draft writer: the whole draft again, as one patch"),
        Node("IC", "Citation compliance: the cited sentences of the patch"),
        Node("SD", "You decide", "stop"),
        Node("SC", "A claim the run cannot support", "stop"),
        Node("CF", PATCHED, "script"),
        Node("F", "Formatter: targets, cover letter, AI disclosure, checklist"),
        Node("WG", WRITING, "maybe"),
    )
    edges = (
        Edge("I", "S"),
        *search_edges,
        Edge("A", "OA"),
        Edge("OA", "A", "restructuring, no cap", "back"),
        Edge("OA", "B"),
        Edge("B", "W"),
        Edge("B", "V"),
        Edge("V", "V", "checks the rendered figure, up to 2 fixes, optional", "back"),
        Edge("W", "C"),
        Edge("W", "AB"),
        Edge("C", "SC", "a claim not held, after every claim is judged"),
        Edge("C", "P"),
        Edge("AB", "P"),
        Edge("V", "P"),
        Edge("P", "WR", "a revision: round 2, once", "dotted"),
        Edge("WR", "IC"),
        Edge("IC", "P", "", "back"),
        Edge("P", "SD", "after round 2 a score under 6.5 with a Critical issue open"),
        Edge("P", "CF"),
        Edge("CF", "F"),
        Edge("F", "F", "a checklist item fails, no cap", "back"),
        Edge("F", "WG"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, edges)


def paper_revision_graph() -> Graph:
    nodes = (
        Node("P", "Peer reviewer: the comments as a roadmap"),
        Node("W", "Draft writer: a patch per item"),
        Node("SD", "You decide", "stop"),
        Node("C", "Citation compliance: each claim against its source's full text"),
        Node("SC", "A claim the run cannot support", "stop"),
        Node("R2", "Peer reviewer: the re-review"),
        Node("W2", "Draft writer: the items not resolved", "maybe"),
        Node("CF", PATCHED, "script"),
        Node("RL", "Draft writer: the response letter, for referee comments", "maybe"),
        Node("RC", "Peer reviewer: the letter checked, a verdict per point", "maybe"),
        Node("WG", WRITING, "maybe"),
        Node("X", "Revised draft, the rest as limitations"),
    )
    edges = (
        Edge("P", "W"),
        Edge("W", "SD", "a structural patch, or a patch rejected twice"),
        Edge("W", "C"),
        Edge("C", "SC", "a claim not held"),
        Edge("C", "R2", "round 2, once"),
        Edge("R2", "W2"),
        Edge("W2", "CF"),
        Edge("R2", "CF"),
        Edge("CF", "RL"),
        Edge("RL", "RC"),
        Edge("RC", "WG"),
        Edge("CF", "WG", "a /writing report as the comments"),
        Edge("WG", "X"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, edges)


def paper_outline_only_graph() -> Graph:
    search_nodes, search_edges = _search("A")
    nodes = (
        Node("I", "Intake agent: you confirm", "stop"),
        *search_nodes,
        Node("A", "Structure architect"),
        Node("OA", "Outline approval", "stop"),
    )
    edges = (
        Edge("I", "S"),
        *search_edges,
        Edge("A", "OA"),
        Edge("OA", "A", "restructuring, no cap", "back"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, edges)


def paper_abstract_only_graph() -> Graph:
    nodes = (
        Node("I", "Intake agent: you confirm", "stop"),
        Node("AB", "Abstract agent: a patch"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, (Edge("I", "AB"),))


def paper_citation_check_graph() -> Graph:
    nodes = (
        Node("C", "Citation compliance: each claim against its source's full text"),
        Node(
            "SS",
            "The citation style to confirm, when more than half the entries fail the same check",
            "stop",
        ),
        Node(
            "SD",
            "A claim the run cannot support, or a reference that cannot be confirmed",
            "stop",
        ),
        Node("X", "Citation report"),
    )
    edges = (
        Edge("C", "SS"),
        Edge("SS", "C", "a fresh start, once", "back"),
        Edge("C", "SD", "a claim not in its source, or a reference no lookup confirms"),
        Edge("C", "X"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, edges)


def paper_disclosure_graph() -> Graph:
    nodes = (
        Node(
            "S",
            "You name the venue, and paste the policy of a venue it does not know",
            "stop",
        ),
        Node("F", "Formatter: each kind of AI use marked, the statement as a patch"),
        Node("U", "You confirm each uncertain kind of AI use", "stop"),
    )
    edges = (
        Edge("S", "F"),
        Edge("F", "U", "a kind marked uncertain"),
        Edge("U", "F", "your answer", "back"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, edges)


def paper_plan_graph() -> Graph:
    nodes = (
        Node("I", "Intake agent: 3 questions, you confirm", "stop"),
        Node("M", "Socratic mentor, chapter by chapter, given each answer"),
        Node("Q", "The mentor's question", "stop"),
        Node("S", "Structure architect: the outline"),
        Node("M2", "Socratic mentor and argument builder: the stress test"),
        Node("X", "Chapter plan and INSIGHT collection"),
    )
    edges = (
        Edge("I", "M"),
        Edge("M", "Q"),
        Edge("Q", "M", "offers after 8 rounds on a chapter, 30 in all", "back"),
        Edge("M", "S"),
        Edge("S", "M2"),
        Edge("M2", "X"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, edges)


def paper_format_convert_graph() -> Graph:
    nodes = (
        Node("F", "Formatter: LaTeX, DOCX, PDF, Markdown, citation style, as patches"),
        Node(
            "F2",
            "Formatter, resumed: the conversion patch, when the citation style changes",
            "maybe",
        ),
        Node("CF", "Script: patches applied, compile, pandoc, the counts", "script"),
        Node("X", "Formatted document"),
    )
    edges = (
        Edge("F", "F2"),
        Edge("F", "CF"),
        Edge("F2", "CF"),
        Edge("CF", "X"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, edges)


def paper_revision_coach_graph() -> Graph:
    nodes = (
        Node("R", "Revision coach: the comments parsed"),
        Node("Q", "You confirm the parsed comments", "stop"),
        Node("R2", "Revision coach, resumed: roadmap, tracking template, letter skeleton"),
    )
    edges = (
        Edge("R", "Q"),
        Edge("Q", "R", "a correction", "back"),
        Edge("Q", "R2"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, edges)


def paper_rebuttal_audit_graph() -> Graph:
    nodes = (
        Node("R", "Revision coach, parsing only: the comments and your response draft"),
        Node("X", "Coverage per comment, gaps, risk flags; advisory, nothing verified"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, (Edge("R", "X"),))


def paper_response_letter_graph() -> Graph:
    nodes = (
        Node("W", "Draft writer: response-letter.md beside the manuscript"),
        Node("P", "Peer reviewer, fresh: a verdict per point"),
        Node("X", "The letter and its check"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, (Edge("W", "P"), Edge("P", "X")))


PAPER_MODES = (
    ("full", paper_full_graph),
    ("revision", paper_revision_graph),
    ("lit-review", paper_lit_review_graph),
    ("outline-only", paper_outline_only_graph),
    ("abstract-only", paper_abstract_only_graph),
    ("citation-check", paper_citation_check_graph),
    ("disclosure", paper_disclosure_graph),
    ("plan", paper_plan_graph),
    ("format-convert", paper_format_convert_graph),
    ("revision-coach", paper_revision_coach_graph),
    ("rebuttal-audit", paper_rebuttal_audit_graph),
    ("response-letter", paper_response_letter_graph),
)
EOF

cd /Users/axelfaes/workspace/ordo/.agents/worktrees/5-paper-5.Q && python3 - <<'EOF'
import re,html
D='docs/figures/academic-paper-comparison/'
def alt(f):
    s=open(D+f).read()
    m=re.search(r'<title>(.*?)</title>',s,re.S); t=html.unescape(m.group(1))
    t=t.replace('[','(').replace(']',')').replace(' (agent)','')
    t=t.replace('. Boxes: ',': ',1)
    return t
modes=['full','revision','lit-review','outline-only','abstract-only','citation-check','disclosure','plan','format-convert','revision-coach','rebuttal-audit','response-letter']
lead="Each mode of `paper` beside the same mode of academic-paper 3.2.0, drawn by `docs/figures/academic-paper-comparison/gen_comparison.py`. The response-letter mode, which academic-paper does not have, is drawn beside steps 3 and 4 of academic-pipeline's external review protocol, which it follows. The simple view draws the agent starts, the stops and the cycles, and the detailed view draws every step, script and stop. One rule holds in every mode and is not drawn: at every patch, a structural patch, a patch that does not apply or a failed script stops the run. Since `paper` has every mode of academic-paper, no mode is left that only academic-paper has."
out=["## paper against academic-paper","",lead,""]
for m in modes:
    out+=[f"### {m}","",f"![{alt(m+'-simple.svg')}](figures/academic-paper-comparison/{m}-simple.svg)","",f"![{alt(m+'-detailed.svg')}](figures/academic-paper-comparison/{m}-detailed.svg)",""]
p='docs/academic-coverage.md'; t=open(p).read()
a=t.index("## paper against academic-paper"); b=t.index("### academic-pipeline and where its stages go")
t=t[:a]+"\n".join(out)+"\n"+t[b:]
open(p,'w').write(t)
EOF

t=open(P).read()
a=t.index("\n## academic-paper\n"); b=t.index("\n## academic-paper-reviewer\n")
sec=t[a:b].split("\n")
out=[]
for line in sec:
    if line.startswith("| `"):
        f=line.split("`")[1]
        if f in AP and AP[f] is not None:
            mark,reason=AP[f]; line=f"| `{f}` | {mark} | {reason} |"
            AP[f]=None
    out.append(line)
left=[k for k,v in AP.items() if v is not None]
assert not left,left
t=t[:a]+"\n".join(out)+t[b:]
open(P,'w').write(t)

110. Write of `docs/adr/0043-paper-s-modes-are-academic-paper-s-copied-with-the-deviations-the-goal-and-the-rulings-keep.md`, the whole file

`````
# 0043. Paper's modes are academic-paper's, copied, with the deviations the goal and the rulings keep

Status: proposed

## Context

Paper's modes other than lit-review had grown a structure of their own beside academic-paper 3.2.0: a planner in place of the structure architect and the argument builder, the draft writer in place of the abstract agent, an intake agent in every mode, stops and agent starts academic-paper does not have, and pages that summarised academic-paper's files instead of holding them.

- Step 5.P made lit-review academic-paper's, with the strategist's page copied and the search left to the session (ADR 0042). In the rc.17 lit-review comparison the judges picked `paper` 4 of 4 against academic-paper (`.scratch/5-paper/agents/reviews/5.P-check-blind-comparison.md`, line 17).
- The same run took 3 h 30 min, 79 min over its bar, and no strategist's brief named a target number of sources (`.scratch/5-paper/agents/reviews/5.P-check-run.md`, line 7).
- The user ruled that every mode of `paper` is academic-paper's, copied and not rewritten, and that a deviation from academic-paper stands only where it makes the output better (`.scratch/5-paper/plan.md`, Rulings: "The rest of paper as academic-paper's", Open items 5.Q-rebuttal, 5.Q-letter-place, 5.Q held, 5.Q-target-modes, 5.Q-entry7 and AD16).

## Decision

1. Each academic-paper file that a `rebuild: paper` row of `docs/academic-coverage.md` names, the four files `workflow_phase_details.md`, `imrad_template.md`, `latex_article_template.tex` and `literature_review_example.md`, and the seven files that were `rebuild: rebuttal` are each copied into one page of `skills/paper/`, section by section with the same words in the same order. A role's page is `references/<role>.md`, every other Markdown file `references/<file name with hyphens>.md`, and the `.tex` template `templates/latex-article-template.tex`. The only edits are these:
   - `paper`'s names for academic-paper's files, roles and records;
   - a sentence on a role, a skill or a mechanism `paper` has no counterpart for, left out;
   - a reference to another academic-paper file named as the page that holds it;
   - the character set and the no-history rule of this repository;
   - the Chinese-language rules left out, the English text kept;
   - a rule that contradicts the manuscript prose standard left out, as ADR 0002 decides.
2. `skills/paper/SKILL.md` is academic-paper's `SKILL.md`, each section carried with its words into the section of `docs/dev/skill-layout.md` that holds its kind of text. Version Info and Version History are left out.
3. Each mode's roles, agent starts, stops and cycles are academic-paper's: twelve roles, the intake agent only in full, outline-only, abstract-only, lit-review and plan, the outline approval, the Generator-Evaluator Contract's four calls in full mode, at most two revision rounds in full and revision mode, and a Critical issue blocking Phase 7. Kept in their place in that structure, since the plan's goal requires them:
   - the LaTeX manuscript and its `.bib`, every change to them a patch applied by `anchorize_tex.py` and `apply_tex_patch.py`, with the compile and its fix, and the rewrite of the whole draft after the peer review as one patch;
   - the claim check against each source's full text in full, revision and citation-check mode, the claim calibration, and the incremental check of the cited sentences of each later patch (ADR 0029);
   - the `lookup.py` reference checks, the `\cite` key check and lit-review's identifier check (ADR 0017);
   - the `/writing` review at the end of full and revision, its question at intake (ADR 0031), and the draft writer's pass over the findings the user picks or its autonomous pass (ADR 0023);
   - academic-paper's mode names under `/paper <mode>`, the run folder (ADR 0020) and the pdfLaTeX engine of the compile.
   The RAISE compliance agent is left out, since `docs/academic-coverage.md` gives it to another skill. Every other choice of `paper` that academic-paper's text does not have is removed.
4. The manuscript prose standard stands in every role's prompt (ADR 0026), and lit-review mode and the strategist's page stay as step 5.P left them.
5. Revision-coach and rebuttal-audit are `paper`'s modes, with the revision coach's page and academic-paper's Rebuttal-Audit Mode section. Rebuttal-audit marks nothing as verified. Revision mode, when its comments are referee comments, ends with the response letter beside the revised manuscript, written by a fresh draft writer, and its check by a fresh peer reviewer with a verdict for each point, as steps 3 and 4 of academic-pipeline's external review protocol give them. `/paper response-letter <main.tex> <comments file>` writes and checks the same letter for a revision already made.
6. Intake has an optional row "Number of sources" in lit-review, full and outline-only mode. Given, the session splits it into one target range per literature strategist start, the ranges summing to the number.

## Alternatives rejected

- Keeping `paper`'s structure and running only the revision and full comparisons again: the structure that judges and the user had already ruled out for lit-review would stay in every other mode, and the result of such a comparison would not show which deviation made the difference.
- Widening step 5.P to every mode: 5.P was built and checked for lit-review alone, and its brief had not read the other modes against academic-paper.

## Consequences

- Each page of `skills/paper/references/` can be read against its source by `diff`, and a new deviation has to be argued against academic-paper's text with a run that shows it is better.
- `paper` has twelve modes and two calls. The pages `ai-disclosure.md`, `latex-template.md`, `modes.md`, `patch-file.md`, `planner.md` and `statements.md` are removed, and their content that the goal keeps stands as `paper`'s addition in the page of the source file whose step it serves.
- Removed from `paper`: the planner, the draft writer's abstract and statements, the intake agent of revision, format-convert, citation-check and disclosure, the unfinished-run stop, the stop on a research question the materials do not answer and the parts of the research question (ADR 0038), the approval of the revision items, the draft-review stops that academic-paper does not have, the anonymous copy, the palette outline, the one stop for every unsupported claim (ADR 0036), the check of each number against the result files (ADR 0028) and the reading of the findings pass (ADR 0029), and the plan's handoff stop.
- `rebuttal`, roadmap entry 7, has no work left, and `docs/academic-coverage.md` lists no `rebuild: rebuttal` row.
- The figures of `docs/figures/paper/` and `docs/figures/academic-paper-comparison/` draw `paper` as built, and `docs/glossary.md`, `docs/academic-coverage.md` and `README.md` describe it.

`````

def ed(p, pairs):

cd /Users/axelfaes/workspace/ordo/.agents/worktrees/5-paper-5.Q/skills/paper && python3 - </dev/null <<'PY'
import re
p='SKILL.md'; t=open(p).read()
a=t.index('## Anti-patterns\n'); b=t.index('## Rules\n')
sec=t[a:b]; lines=sec.split('\n'); out=[]
for l in lines:
    if l.startswith('| # | Anti-Pattern'): out.append('| Anti-pattern | Why it fails | Do instead |'); continue
    if l.startswith('|---|'): out.append('|---|---|---|'); continue
    m=re.match(r'^\| \d+ \| (.*)$', l)
    if m: out.append('| '+m.group(1)); continue
    out.append(l)
t=t[:a]+'\n'.join(out)+t[b:]
b=t.index('## Rules\n'); rest=t[b:]
# end of Rules = next '## ' heading or EOF
m=re.search(r'\n## ', rest[3:]); end=b+3+m.start()+1 if m else len(t)
r=t[b:end]
rep=[
("1. **Every claim must have a citation** or be supported by the paper's own data\n2. **Zero citation orphans**: in-text citations <-> reference list must perfectly match\n3. **Consistent register**: academic tone appropriate for the discipline\n4. **Logical flow**: clear transitions between paragraphs and sections\n5. **Word count compliance**: within +/-10% of target",

("10. **Format compliance**: 100% adherence to selected citation style\n11. IRON RULE: **DOI inclusion**: every source with a DOI must include it; every citation must be verified via DOI or a `lookup.py` search\n12. **Currency**: flag sources older than 10 years (unless seminal works)\n13. **Self-citation ratio**: flag if >15%",
 "- **Format compliance**: 100% adherence to selected citation style\n- IRON RULE: **DOI inclusion**: every source with a DOI must include it.\n- IRON RULE: every citation must be verified via DOI or a `lookup.py` search.\n- **Currency**: flag sources older than 10 years (unless seminal works)\n- **Self-citation ratio**: flag if >15%"),

142. Bash, "Rewrite the figure descriptions in README and the coverage page"

`````
cd /Users/axelfaes/workspace/ordo/.agents/worktrees/5-paper-5.Q && python3 - </dev/null <<'PY'
import re,html,sys
sys.path.insert(0,'docs/figures')
import gen_figures as gf
cov=open('docs/academic-coverage.md').read().split('\n'); n=0
for i,l in enumerate(cov):
    m=re.match(r'^!\[(.*)\]\((figures/academic-paper-comparison/([^)]*-(?:simple|detailed)\.svg))\)$',l)
    if not m: continue
    svg=open('docs/'+m.group(2)).read()
    t=html.unescape(re.search(r'<title>(.*?)</title>',svg,re.S).group(1))
    alt=t.replace('. Boxes: ',': ').replace(' (agent)','').replace('[','(').replace(']',')')
    cov[i]=f'![{alt}]({m.group(2)})'; n+=1
open('docs/academic-coverage.md','w').write('\n'.join(cov)); print('coverage',n)
K={"agent":"","maybe":", dashed","stop":", a stop","script":", a grey box for a script the session runs","choice":", a rounded box where the session judges"}
rd=open('README.md').read().split('\n'); n=0
for mode,build in gf.PAPER_MODES:
    g=gf.with_mode_stop(build())
    alt=f"The {mode} mode of paper: "+"; ".join(x.label+K[x.kind] for x in (*g.nodes,*g.floating))+"."
    path=f"docs/figures/paper/{mode}.svg"
    idx=[i for i,l in enumerate(rd) if l.startswith('![') and l.endswith(f']({path})')]
    assert len(idx)==1,mode
    rd[idx[0]]=f'![{alt}]({path})'; n+=1
open('README.md','w').write('\n'.join(rd)); print('readme',n)
PY

`````
cd /Users/axelfaes/workspace/ordo/.agents/worktrees/5-paper-5.Q && python3 - </dev/null <<'PY'
import glob
p=glob.glob('docs/adr/0043-*.md')[0]; t=open(p).read()
pairs=[
("In the rc.17 lit-review comparison the judges picked `paper` 4 of 4 against academic-paper (`.scratch/5-paper/agents/reviews/5.P-check-blind-comparison.md`, line 17).",
 "In the rc.17 lit-review comparison the judges picked `paper` 4 of 4 against academic-paper (`.scratch/5-paper/agents/reviews/5.P-check-blind-comparison.md`, section \"Lit-review\")."),
("- The same run took 3 h 30 min, 79 min over its bar, and no strategist's brief named a target number of sources (`.scratch/5-paper/agents/reviews/5.P-check-run.md`, line 7).",
 "- The same run took 3 h 30 min, 79 min over its bar, and no strategist's brief named a target number of sources (`.scratch/5-paper/agents/reviews/5.P-check-run.md`, section \"The slower stage and its cause\")."),
("""3. Each mode's roles, agent starts, stops and cycles are academic-paper's: twelve roles, the intake agent only in full, outline-only, abstract-only, lit-review and plan, the outline approval, the Generator-Evaluator Contract's four calls in full mode, at most two revision rounds in full and revision mode, and a Critical issue blocking Phase 7. Kept in their place in that structure, since the plan's goal requires them:
   - the LaTeX manuscript and its `.bib`, every change to them a patch applied by `anchorize_tex.py` and `apply_tex_patch.py`, with the compile and its fix, and the rewrite of the whole draft after the peer review as one patch;
   - the claim check against each source's full text in full, revision and citation-check mode, the claim calibration, and the incremental check of the cited sentences of each later patch (ADR 0029);
   - the `lookup.py` reference checks, the `\\cite` key check and lit-review's identifier check (ADR 0017);
   - the `/writing` review at the end of full and revision, its question at intake (ADR 0031), and the draft writer's pass over the findings the user picks or its autonomous pass (ADR 0023);
   - academic-paper's mode names under `/paper <mode>`, the run folder (ADR 0020) and the pdfLaTeX engine of the compile.
   The RAISE compliance agent is left out, since `docs/academic-coverage.md` gives it to another skill. Every other choice of `paper` that academic-paper's text does not have is removed.""",
"""3. Each mode's roles, agent starts, stops and cycles are academic-paper's: twelve roles, the intake agent only in full, outline-only, abstract-only, lit-review and plan, the outline approval, the Generator-Evaluator Contract's four calls in full mode, at most two revision rounds in full and revision mode, and a Critical issue blocking Phase 7. Each part below is kept in its place in that structure, since the plan's goal requires it:
   - The LaTeX manuscript and its `.bib` are the manuscript's form, since the goal runs every mode on a LaTeX manuscript and its `.bib`.
   - Every change to the manuscript or its `.bib` is a patch applied by `anchorize_tex.py` and `apply_tex_patch.py`, since the goal moves the two scripts in for the revision patches.
   - The compile and its fix follow the patches, since a patch that breaks the LaTeX is otherwise found only by the user.
   - Academic-paper's rewrite of the whole draft after the peer review is made as one patch, since every change is a patch.
   - The claim check against each source's full text runs in full, revision and citation-check mode, as the goal names it.
   - The claim calibration with `calibration_counts.py` is kept, since it is part of the claim check.
   - The citation check of the cited sentences of each later patch is kept (ADR 0029), since a cited sentence a later patch adds is otherwise never checked and the claim check would not hold for the finished manuscript.
   - The `lookup.py` check of every reference the skill keeps, every other reference lookup and the `\\cite` key check are kept (ADR 0017), as the goal names them.
   - Lit-review's identifier check is kept, since it is the `lookup.py` check of the references lit-review keeps.
   - The `/writing` review at the end of full and revision, with the findings the user accepts applied, is kept, since the goal applies the `/writing` findings the user accepts.
   - The draft writer's pass that judges and applies the `/writing` findings when the user asks is kept (ADR 0023), as the goal names it.
   - Intake's question whether full and revision end with `/writing` is kept (ADR 0031), asked by the session in revision, which has no intake agent, since the review is the user's choice.
   - Academic-paper's mode names stand under `/paper <mode>`, as the goal names them.
   - The run folder with `config.md` is kept (ADR 0020), since the records of these checks are written there.
   - The compile's engine is pdfLaTeX, since the scripts and the compile need one engine named.
   The RAISE compliance agent is left out, since `docs/academic-coverage.md` gives it to another skill. Every other choice of `paper` that academic-paper's text does not have is removed, except the deviations below, each kept for the reason given and under the ruling named:
   - Four refusals end a call before any agent starts: "Unknown mode", "An argument that does not exist", "The `.bib` is not one file" and "No prose standard". `docs/dev/skill-layout.md` asks that an input whose absence is a refusal says so; the third follows from the LaTeX manuscript and its `.bib`, and the fourth from Decision 4.
   - In the modes with no intake agent (revision, response-letter, format-convert, citation-check, disclosure, revision-coach, rebuttal-audit and the two calls) the session asks the user, in one message, only what the mode's items name, as academic-paper's modes without an intake agent take what they need from the call. Revision asks the rows Closing review, Claims not to make and Earlier papers, and citation-check the rows Claims not to make and Earlier papers.
   - The intake row Claims not to make, asked in full and revision and by the session in citation-check, is read by the citation compliance agent, which flags each sentence that states such a claim (ruling Open item AI2), since academic-paper's claim intent manifest carries these negative constraints and the claim check otherwise does not see them.
   - The intake row Earlier papers, asked in full and revision and by the session in citation-check, is read by the citation compliance agent, which flags the passages that closely repeat the authors' earlier papers (ruling Open item AD8), as academic-paper's citation compliance agent does.
   - A negative or null result is never written up, with one exception: a manuscript the user asks for as internal only, which the intake row Internal only records and the intake confirmation shows (ruling Open item AT), since the user does not publish such a result and an internal record of every attempt is asked for by name.
   - The calls `draft-review` and `figures` run the peer reviewer and the visualization agent on an existing manuscript (ruling Open item AD14), as academic-paper's Phase-by-phase Invocation Contract runs one role per call.
   - The stop "The citation style to confirm" is raised when the citation compliance agent finds that more than half the entries fail the same check (ruling AV14), as academic-paper's failure path F5 confirms the citation format with the user before fixing each entry.
   - The stop "A reference that cannot be confirmed" is raised only under strict, as academic-paper's intake question gives it; under mark only the reference is marked in `citation-report.md` and named in the final message.
   - The response letter, its check and the mode `response-letter` stand as Decision 5 gives them (rulings Open item 5.Q-rebuttal and 5.Q-letter-place), since academic-paper has no step that answers referee comments with a letter and academic-pipeline's external review protocol gives both steps.
   - The intake row Number of sources stands as Decision 6 gives it (rulings Open item 5.Q held and 5.Q-target-modes), since no strategist's brief in the 5.P check run named a target number of sources and that run went 79 min over its bar."""),
]
for o,n in pairs:
    assert t.count(o)==1,o[:60]; t=t.replace(o,n)
open(p,'w').write(t)

### The scratchpad files those commands run

The commands above run these files by path (`python3 -` with the file as input, or `from ed import ed`). Each is quoted whole.

`ed.py`:

````
import sys
R = '/Users/axelfaes/workspace/ordo/.agents/worktrees/5-paper-5.Q/skills/paper/'
def ed(path, pairs):
    p = R + path
    t = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read()
    for a, b in pairs:
        n = t.count(a)
        if n != 1:
            raise SystemExit(f"{path}: {a[:80]!r} occurs {n} times")
        t = t.replace(a, b)
    open(p, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(t)
    if open(p, encoding='utf-8').read() != t:
        raise SystemExit(f"{path}: read back differs")
    print(f"{path}: {len(pairs)} edits")
````

`charmap-cmd.txt`:

````
python3 - $(cat pages.txt) <<'PY'
import re, sys
BOX = {'<U+2500>':'-','<U+2501>':'-','<U+2550>':'=','<U+2502>':'|','<U+251C>':'+','<U+2514>':'+','<U+250C>':'+','<U+2510>':'+','<U+2518>':'+','<U+2524>':'+','<U+252C>':'+','<U+2534>':'+','<U+253C>':'+'}
SIMPLE = {'<U+2260>':'!=','<U+2265>':'>=','<U+2264>':'<=','<U+00D7>':'x','<U+2208>':'in','<U+00B1>':'+/-','<U+2229>':'&','<U+21D2>':'=>','<U+25A1>':'[ ]','<U+2610>':'[ ]','<U+2713>':'[x]','<U+2026>':'...','<U+03B7>':'eta','<U+00B9>':'^1','<U+00B2>':'^2','<U+2074>':'^4','<U+2075>':'^5','<U+00B6>':'paragraph'}
def dash(line):
    line = re.sub(r'\|\s*<U+2014>\s*(?=\|)', '| - ', line)
    line = re.sub(r'(\d)<U+2013>(\d)', r'\1-\2', line)
    line = line.replace(' <U+2013> ', ' <U+2014> ').replace('<U+2013>', '-')
    if re.match(r'\s*(#+ |[-*] |\d+\. |\*\*)', line) and ' <U+2014> ' in line:
        head, tail = line.split(' <U+2014> ', 1)
        if len(head.split()) <= 12:
            line = head + ': ' + tail
    line = re.sub(r'\s*<U+2014>\s*', ', ', line)
    line = line.replace(', ,', ',').replace(',,', ',').replace(':,', ':').replace('(, ', '(')
    return line
for path in sys.argv[1:]:
    text = open(path, encoding='utf-8').read()
    out, code = [], False
    for line in text.split('\n'):
        if line.lstrip().startswith('```'):
            code = not code
        for k, v in BOX.items():
            line = line.replace(k, v)
        line = re.sub('[<U+26A0><U+2605>]<U+FE0F>? ?', '', line).replace(' NEW', ' NEW') if ('<U+26A0>' in line or '<U+2605>' in line) else line
        line = line.replace('<U+00A7><U+00A7>', 'sections ').replace('<U+00A7> ', 'section ').replace('<U+00A7>', 'section ')
        for k, v in SIMPLE.items():
            line = line.replace(k, v)
        if code:
            line = line.replace('<U+2192>', '->').replace('<U+2190>', '<-').replace('<U+2193>', 'v').replace('<U+2194>', '<->')
        else:
            line = re.sub(r'\s*<U+2192>\s*', ' to ', line)
        line = dash(line)
        out.append(line)
    new = '\n'.join(out)
    open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(new)
    if open(path, encoding='utf-8').read() != new:
        sys.exit(path + ': read back differs')
PY
````

`rolemap-cmd.txt`:

````
python3 - <<'PY'
import re
SP = '/private/tmp/claude-502/-Users-axelfaes-workspace-ordo/e68b9e7f-9bfe-4218-be15-6dd18d5f22c0/scratchpad/'
R = '/Users/axelfaes/workspace/ordo/.agents/worktrees/5-paper-5.Q/skills/paper/'
ROLE = {'intake':'intake agent','literature_strategist':'literature strategist','structure_architect':'structure architect','argument_builder':'argument builder','draft_writer':'draft writer','citation_compliance':'citation compliance agent','abstract_bilingual':'abstract agent','peer_reviewer':'peer reviewer','formatter':'formatter','socratic_mentor':'Socratic mentor','visualization':'visualization agent','revision_coach':'revision coach','format':'formatter'}
alt = '|'.join(sorted(ROLE, key=len, reverse=True))
rows = [l.rstrip('\n').split('\t') for l in open(SP + 'map.tsv')]
def cap(s): return s[0].upper() + s[1:]
for s, p in rows:
    path = R + p
    t = open(path, encoding='utf-8').read()
    o = t
    t = re.sub(r'^name: (' + alt + r')_agent$', lambda m: 'name: ' + p.split('/')[-1][:-3], t, flags=re.M)
    t = re.sub(r'\| `(' + alt + r')_agent`', lambda m: '| ' + cap(ROLE[m.group(1)]), t)
    t = re.sub(r'\[(' + alt + r')_agent\]', lambda m: '[' + ROLE[m.group(1)] + ']', t)
    t = re.sub(r'\[(' + alt + r')_agent \+ (' + alt + r')_agent\]', lambda m: '[' + ROLE[m.group(1)] + ' + ' + ROLE[m.group(2)] + ']', t)
    t = re.sub(r'(?<=^  - )(' + alt + r')_agent$', lambda m: ROLE[m.group(1)], t, flags=re.M)
    def prose(m):
        pre, role = m.group(1), ROLE[m.group(2)]
        if re.search(r'(?i)\bthe $', pre):
            return pre + role
        start = pre == '' or re.search(r'(^|[.!?]\s|^\s*(?:[-*]|\d+\.)\s|\*\*|^#+ |\(|: )$', pre)
        return pre + ('The ' if start and not pre.endswith('(') and not pre.endswith(': ') else 'the ') + role
    t = re.sub(r'(^[^\n]*?|)`?\b(' + alt + r')_agent\b`?', lambda m: prose(m), t, flags=re.M) if False else t
    out = []
    for line in t.split('\n'):
        while True:
            m = re.search(r'`?\b(' + alt + r')_agent\b`?', line)
            if not m: break
            pre = line[:m.start()]
            role = ROLE[m.group(1)]
            if re.search(r'(?i)\bthe $', pre):
                rep = role
            elif re.search(r'(^\s*$|[.!?]\s+$|^\s*(?:[-*>]|\d+\.)\s+(\*\*)?$|^\s*\*\*$|^#+ $)', pre):
                rep = 'The ' + role
            else:
                rep = 'the ' + role
            line = pre + rep + line[m.end():]
        out.append(line)
    t = '\n'.join(out)
    if t != o:
        open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(t)
        assert open(path, encoding='utf-8').read() == t
        print(p)
PY
````

`refmap-cmd.txt`:

````
python3 - <<'PY'
import re
SP = '/private/tmp/claude-502/-Users-axelfaes-workspace-ordo/e68b9e7f-9bfe-4218-be15-6dd18d5f22c0/scratchpad/'
R = '/Users/axelfaes/workspace/ordo/.agents/worktrees/5-paper-5.Q/skills/paper/'
rows = [l.rstrip('\n').split('\t') for l in open(SP + 'map.tsv')]
names = {s.split('/')[-1]: p for s, p in rows}
names['literature_strategist_agent.md'] = 'references/literature-strategist.md'
alt = '|'.join(re.escape(n) for n in sorted(names, key=len, reverse=True))
pat = re.compile(r'(?:\.\./)*(?:academic-paper/|academic-paper-reviewer/|academic-pipeline/)?(?:(?:references|agents|templates|examples)/)?(' + alt + r')(?![A-Za-z0-9_])')
total = 0
for s, p in rows:
    path = R + p
    t = open(path, encoding='utf-8').read()
    new, n = pat.subn(lambda m: names[m.group(1)], t)
    if n:
        open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(new)
        assert open(path, encoding='utf-8').read() == new
        print(p, n)
        total += n
print('total', total)
PY

`zh-cmd.txt`:

python3 - references/funding-statement-guide.md references/funding-statement-template.md references/credit-statement-template.md references/credit-authorship-guide.md references/literature-review-example.md <<'PY'
import re,sys
for p in sys.argv[1:]:
    t=open(p).read(); o=t
    t=re.sub(r'\n\*\*(?:[A-Za-z ]+ )?\(?Chinese(?: Format Example| Version| Template)?\)?\*\*:?\n+```[a-z]*\n.*?\n```\n', '\n', t, flags=re.S)
    t=re.sub(r'\n\*\*No Conflict of Interest\*\* \(Chinese\):\n```\n.*?\n```\n', '\n', t, flags=re.S)
    t=re.sub(r'^\|[^\n]*Chinese format[^\n]*\|\n', '', t, flags=re.M)
    t=re.sub(r'^- \[ \] English papers use English statements; Chinese papers use Chinese statements\n', '', t, flags=re.M)
    t=t.replace('3. **Both Chinese and English declarations required**: If the paper is in English, use English for the funding statement; if in Chinese, use Chinese\n4. **Open Access fees**', '3. **Open Access fees**')
    if '## Chinese Abstract' in t:
        a=t.index('## Chinese Abstract'); b=t.index('## 1. Introduction')
        t=t[:a]+t[b:]
    t=re.sub(r'\n{3,}', '\n\n', t)
    if t!=o: open(p,'w').write(t); print(p)

python3 - references/*.md <<'PY'
import re,sys
for p in sys.argv[1:]:
    L=open(p).read().split('\n'); out=[]; f=False; n=0
    for b in L:
        if b.lstrip().startswith(('```','~~~')):
            f=not f; out.append(b); continue
        if not f and out:
            a=out[-1]
            ok = a.strip() and b.strip() and len(a)>=70 and not a.lstrip().startswith(('|','#','>','<!--','---','```')) \
                 and not b.lstrip().startswith(('|','#','>','```','<!--')) and not re.match(r'\s*([-*+]|\d+[.)]|- \[[ x]\]) ',b) \
                 and not a.rstrip().endswith(('  ','\\'))
            if ok:
                out[-1]=a.rstrip()+' '+b.strip(); n+=1; continue
        out.append(b)
    if n: open(p,'w').write('\n'.join(out)); print(p,n)
PY
````

import re,sys
for p in sys.argv[1:]:

    t=re.sub(r'^\*\*Status\*\*:.*\n','',t,flags=re.M)
    t=t.replace('**Parent skill**: `academic-paper`','**Parent skill**: `paper`')
    t=t.replace("ARS's v3.2 `disclosure` mode","`paper`'s `disclosure` mode").replace('ARS does not','`paper` does not').replace('What ARS did','What `paper` did')
    t=re.sub(r' ?\((?:same as )?v3\.2(?:, default)?\)', lambda m: ' (default)' if 'default' in m.group(0) else '', t)
    t=re.sub(r' ?\(#108(?: path| extension[^)]*)?\)','',t)
    t=re.sub(r' ?\((?:v3\.2 )?path, default\)',' (default)',t)
    t=t.replace("v3.2's venue track",'the venue track').replace('v3.2 venue path','the venue path').replace('v3.2 venue track','the venue track').replace('v3.2 path','venue path')
    t=re.sub(r'\bv3\.2 ','',t)
    t=re.sub(r'\b#108 ','',t)
    t=re.sub(r' ?\(#108 \+ v3\.2\)','',t)
    t=t.replace('`shared/policy_data/nature_policy.md`','the Nature policy text of `references/venue-disclosure-policies.md`')

python3 - references/policy-anchor-disclosure-protocol.md references/policy-anchor-table.md <<'PY'
import re,sys
for p in sys.argv[1:]:
    t=open(p).read(); o=t
    t=re.sub(r'^.*is mirrored by the conformance referee.*$', lambda m: m.group(0).split(' The recognition logic above is mirrored')[0], t, flags=re.M)
    t=re.sub(r'^- Shared Nature policy source.*\n','',t,flags=re.M)
    t=re.sub(r' ?\(concern #\d+(?: resolution)?\)','',t)
    t=re.sub(r'\(concern #\d+ resolution; ','(',t)
    t=re.sub(r' \(concern #\d+ resolution\)','',t)
    t=t.replace('applying the **concern #6 resolution**','applying the **per-facet rule**').replace("behind concern #6's resolution","behind the per-facet rule").replace('per concern #6 resolution','per the per-facet rule').replace('concern #5 resolution = ','').replace('**Concern #10 resolution (','**Substantive-content gate (')
    t=t.replace('Per section 4.3 G7 invariant + section 4.5 / section 4.6 cell #15,','Per the G7 invariant,').replace('(section 4.3 G3 / G10 invariant)','(G3 / G10 invariant)').replace('The section 4.3 invariants constrain','The invariants constrain').replace('This table is the section 4.3 ','This table is the ').replace('The section 4.3 G7 invariant','The G7 invariant')
    t=t.replace('**Forbidden by Decision Doc section 4.3 (renderer must honour these):**','**Forbidden (renderer must honour these):**').replace('(Decision Doc section 4.3 G8 invariant)','(G8 invariant)').replace('via the section 4.4 #5 hybrid output channel','via the hybrid output channel')
    t=re.sub(r'^\*\*Provenance\*\*:.*\n','',t,flags=re.M)
    t=re.sub(r'^\*\*Lint contract\*\*:.*\n','',t,flags=re.M)
    t=re.sub(r'^- (?:Decision Doc \(parent\)|Implementation spec \(parent\)|Discovery doc).*\n','',t,flags=re.M)
    t=t.replace('the renderer routes to this anchor only when the upstream pipeline (or cold-start `mode=` input) sets `slr_lineage=true`.','the renderer routes to this anchor only when the `mode=systematic-review` input selects it.')
    if t!=o: open(p,'w').write(t); print(p)
PY

import re
S='/Users/axelfaes/workspace/ordo/.agents/worktrees/5-paper-5.Q/skills/paper/SKILL.md'
old=open('old-format-convert.sec').read()
lead="Text formats: LaTeX (.tex + .bib), DOCX (via Pandoc), PDF (via LaTeX or Pandoc), Markdown. Citation formats: APA 7.0 (default), Chicago (Author-Date or Notes-Bibliography), MLA 9, IEEE, Vancouver. The formatter supports late-stage citation format conversion between any two supported formats via \"Convert citations to [format]\".\n\n"
E=[
("an unfinished run, the intake confirmation, a structural","a structural"),
("Intake (Steps 6) asks the rows `references/intake.md` marks for format-convert.","Format-convert has no intake agent. The session asks the user, in one message, for what the items below name and the call does not give, and writes the answers into `config.md` (Steps 6): the targets, the Format Profile of `references/intake.md`, Step 5, and the Existing Materials, such as a reference `.docx`, a CSL file or a venue's template."),
("1. Settle the targets at intake.","1. Settle the targets from the call and the session's question (Steps 6)."),
("A request with no target takes Markdown, and the intake confirmation shows it.","A call with no target takes Markdown, and the session's question names it."),
("4. Start the formatter (Steps 8 and 9) the first time.","4. Start the formatter (Steps 8 and 9)."),
("It reads these rows of `config.md`: Target formats, Citation style, Venue, Venue limits, Cover letter, Format profile, Materials in hand, Paper type and the Notes.","It reads these rows of `config.md`: Output Format, Citation Format, Target Journal, Format Profile, Existing Materials and Paper Type."),
("When it writes a cover letter it also reads the rows Authors, Human or patient data and AI use. In lit-review mode it reads the row Authors (`references/formatter.md`, \"What the agent reads\").","When it writes a cover letter it also reads the row Co-Authors (`references/formatter.md`, \"Cover Letter Generation\")."),
("Item 6 starts the formatter again for them, after the compile of the new style.","Item 6 resumes the formatter for them, after the compile of the new style."),
("compile the copy and start the formatter again for the conversion patch.","compile the copy and resume the formatter for the conversion patch."),
("Start the formatter (Steps 8 and 9) a second time, as a fresh agent. It is given `config.md`, the files of `convert/` with their manifests and patch paths, the compile's `.aux` and `.bbl`, the first start's `format-report.md` and the path of `convert/`.","Resume the formatter (Steps 8). It is given the files of `convert/` with their manifests and patch paths, the compile's `.aux` and `.bbl` and the path of `convert/`."),
("The intake confirmation names each such file","The session's question (Steps 6) names each such file"),
("is replaced only after the intake confirmation has named it.","is replaced only after the session's question (Steps 6) has named it and the user has confirmed it."),
("   - It lists each field a limit of the Venue limits row made the venue's value (field, the limit, the value overridden).\n",""),
]
for o,n in E:
    c=old.count(o)
    assert c>=1,(o,c)
    old=old.replace(o,n)
old=old.replace("Materials in hand","Existing Materials").replace("Format profile row","Format Profile row")
txt=open(S).read()
a=txt.index("### format-convert\n")+len("### format-convert\n")
b=txt.index("### citation-check\n")
txt=txt[:a]+"\n"+lead+old.lstrip("\n")+("" if old.endswith("\n\n") else "\n")+txt[b:]
open(S,'w').write(txt)
````

def held(page): return f"{CP}, held in `skills/paper/{page}`."
AP={
"SKILL.md":("rebuild: paper","The file sets twelve agents, eleven modes, the eight phases with their checkpoint rules, the Generator-Evaluator Contract of full mode, the revision patch protocol, the rebuttal audit, the failure paths, the anti-patterns and the quality standards. Every section is carried into the layout of `skills/paper/SKILL.md` with its words: the agent team in Agent prompts, the modes and the selection guide in Steps, one heading per mode, the checkpoint rules in Stops, the quality standards and the output language in Rules. `paper` keeps the LaTeX manuscript changed only by patches, the claim check against each source's full text, the `lookup.py` reference checks, the run folder and the `/writing` review at the end of full and revision, and adds the response-letter mode. The zh-TW triggers and the bilingual abstract, the RAISE compliance agent, Mode A of `academic-pipeline`, the version notes and the integration list are left out. The research stage and the integrity check of a finished manuscript go to `researcher`, the external review to `paper-review`."),
"agents/abstract_bilingual_agent.md":("rebuild: paper","Language-neutral rules ask for five parts, 150 to 300 words, five to seven keywords outside the title, no citations and defined abbreviations. "+CP+", the zh-TW abstract and keywords left out, held in `skills/paper/references/abstract-bilingual.md`. The abstract agent writes the abstract and the keywords as one patch in full and abstract-only mode, held in `skills/paper/SKILL.md`."),
"agents/argument_builder_agent.md":("rebuild: paper","A thesis rests on three to five claim-evidence-reasoning chains with counter-arguments and rebuttals, and two weak-argument signs return the argument for strengthening. "+CP+", held in `skills/paper/references/argument-builder.md`. It runs in Phase 3 of full mode and in the stress test and chapter plan of plan mode, held in `skills/paper/SKILL.md`."),
"agents/citation_compliance_agent.md":("rebuild: paper","Citations and references are checked both ways and for DOI form, self-citation, age and retractions, and a claim not in its source stops the check. "+CP+", held in `skills/paper/references/citation-compliance.md`. `paper`'s addition stands beside it in the same page (\"Paper's addition: the lookups and the claim check\"): the `lookup.py` checks of every reference, each claim judged against the passage read in its source's full text, one stop for every claim the run cannot support, the incremental check of each later patch, the calibration and the claims against the draft writer's list. Chinese citation rules stay out."),
"agents/draft_writer_agent.md":("rebuild: paper","The writer drafts sections in topic-evidence-explanation-link paragraphs, cites sources predating the events they evidence, and revises by hashed block patches. "+CP+", held in `skills/paper/references/draft-writer.md`, with its Generator-Evaluator Contract sub-sections for Phase 4a and 4b. `paper`'s addition in the same page (\"Paper's addition: the LaTeX manuscript\") holds the LaTeX draft, the patch rules, the reference fix, the compile fix and the pass over the `/writing` findings, with `skills/paper/templates/anchorize_tex.py` and `skills/paper/templates/apply_tex_patch.py`."),
"agents/formatter_agent.md":("rebuild: paper","The formatter converts to LaTeX, DOCX, PDF and Markdown, applies journal formatting and format profiles with venue over user precedence, writes the cover letter and the AI disclosure and runs the final checklist. "+CP+", held in `skills/paper/references/formatter.md`. `paper`'s addition in the same page (\"Paper's addition: the LaTeX manuscript\") holds the patches of format-convert, the conversion patch for `pandoc` and the `.md` or `.docx` given. The session converts and counts, with `skills/paper/templates/reference_docx.py` for the DOCX style map, held in `skills/paper/SKILL.md` (\"format-convert\"). The provenance gates, the ARS marker stripping, the zh-TW LaTeX and Chinese Pandoc settings are left out. `submit-manuscript` takes the submission checks."),
"agents/intake_agent.md":("rebuild: paper","Intake fixes type, discipline, venue, citation style, output format, language, length, materials, co-authors, funding, style calibration, the evidence profile and the citation-verification level, and writes the configuration record for confirmation. "+CP+", the zh-TW options left out, held in `skills/paper/references/intake.md`. `paper` adds the rows Number of sources, Claims not to make, Earlier papers, Internal only and Closing review (\"Step 14\"). The modes academic-paper runs without an intake agent have the session ask what they need, held in `skills/paper/SKILL.md`, Steps 6."),
"agents/literature_strategist_agent.md":None,
"agents/peer_reviewer_agent.md":("rebuild: paper","The reviewer scores five weighted dimensions into a verdict, lists issues with severity and fix, re-reviews flagged items for two rounds, and in full mode runs as the evaluator of the Generator-Evaluator Contract. "+CP+", held in `skills/paper/references/peer-reviewer.md`. `paper`'s addition in the same page (\"Paper's addition: the response letter check\") checks a response letter against the revised manuscript. The panel of `paper-review` is built from academic-paper-reviewer."),
"agents/revision_coach_agent.md":("rebuild: paper","The coach parses reviewer comments without dropping any, splits compound ones, classifies and maps each, and extracts each promise with its evidence. The user sees the parse first. "+CP+", held in `skills/paper/references/revision-coach.md`, with the commitments written to `commitments.md` in the run folder in place of Schema 11. It runs in revision-coach and rebuttal-audit mode, held in `skills/paper/SKILL.md`."),
"agents/socratic_mentor_agent.md":("rebuild: paper","The planning dialogue runs a readiness check, the thesis, the chapters with convergence caps, contribution sharpening and the stress test with the argument builder. "agents/structure_architect_agent.md":("rebuild: paper","The paper type picks a structure whose outline gives each section a purpose and a word count within 5 percent, maps every source and adds transitions. "agents/visualization_agent.md":("rebuild: paper","A decision tree picks the chart type; the figure standards set 300 dpi and colourblind-safe palettes, and the pitfalls rule out dual or truncated axes and missing error bars. "templates/imrad_template.md":("rebuild: paper","The IMRaD skeleton runs from context and gap through design, measures and validity to results per question and a discussion with limitations. "+held("references/imrad-template.md")),
"templates/latex_article_template.tex":("rebuild: paper","This generic APA-style `article` template sets double spacing, Times, natbib `apalike` and a commented zh-TW abstract. The file is copied with its lines, its Chinese-character lines left out, held in `skills/paper/templates/latex-article-template.tex`."),
"templates/literature_review_template.md":("rebuild: paper","A review opens with scope and method, summarises each theme, synthesises agreements and debates, and ends with gaps and an agenda. "+held("references/literature-review-template.md")),
"templates/policy_brief_template.md":("rebuild: paper","A policy brief for decision-makers scores options on effectiveness, feasibility, cost and political acceptability and ends with implementation steps and success metrics. "+held("references/policy-brief-template.md")),
"templates/revision_tracking_template.md":("rebuild: paper","Each comment gets a row with the change location and one of four statuses, needing a location, justification, constraint or evidence-based rebuttal. "+CP+", the ledger mapped to `commitments.md` and the response letter check, held in `skills/paper/references/revision-tracking-template.md`."),
"templates/theoretical_paper_template.md":("rebuild: paper","A theory paper traces a theory's origins, critiques it and proposes an extension with testable propositions. "+held("references/theoretical-paper-template.md")),
}
t=open(P).read()
a=t.index("\n## academic-paper\n"); b=t.index("\n## academic-paper-reviewer\n")
sec=t[a:b].split("\n")
out=[]
for line in sec:
    if line.startswith("| `"):
        f=line.split("`")[1]
        if f in AP and AP[f] is not None:
            mark,reason=AP[f]; line=f"| `{f}` | {mark} | {reason} |"
            AP[f]=None
    out.append(line)
left=[k for k,v in AP.items() if v is not None]
assert not left,left
t=t[:a]+"\n".join(out)+t[b:]
open(P,'w').write(t)
````

`fig/simple.py`:

````
SEARCH_STOP = "search rounds: end at any 3 of 5 conditions, or after 4 rounds"
PATCHED = "Script: each patch applied, the compile, the reference checks"
WRITING = "/writing review, unless you said no; the draft writer applies what you pick"


def _search(after: str) -> tuple[tuple[Node, ...], tuple[Edge, ...]]:
    """The session's choice of one literature strategist or several with a merge."""
    nodes = (
        Node("S", STRUCTURE, "choice"),
        Node("L", "Literature strategist, unless you give your own sources", "maybe"),
        Node("T2", STARTS, "maybe"),
        Node("M", MERGE + "review.md and refs.bib", "maybe"),
        Node("LR", "You review the sources, optional", "stop"),
    )
    edges = (
        Edge("S", "L", ONE),
        Edge("S", "T2", SEVERAL),
        Edge("L", "L", SEARCH_STOP, "back"),
        Edge("T2", "T2", SEARCH_STOP, "back"),
        Edge("T2", "M"),
        Edge("L", "LR"),
        Edge("M", "LR"),
        Edge("LR", after),
    )
    return nodes, edges


def paper_full_graph() -> Graph:
    search_nodes, search_edges = _search("A")
    nodes = (
        Node("I", "Intake agent: you confirm", "stop"),
        *search_nodes,
        Node("A", "Structure architect"),
        Node("OA", "Outline approval", "stop"),
        Node("B", "Argument builder"),
        Node("W", "Draft writer, 2 calls: main.tex"),
        Node("V", "Visualization, with data: the figures placed by patch", "maybe"),
        Node("C", "Citation compliance: each claim against its source's full text"),
        Node("AB", "Abstract agent: a patch"),
        Node("P", "Peer reviewer, 2 calls"),
        Node("WR", "Draft writer: the whole draft again, as one patch"),
        Node("IC", "Citation compliance: the cited sentences of the patch"),
        Node("SD", "You decide", "stop"),
        Node("SC", "A claim the run cannot support", "stop"),
        Node("CF", PATCHED, "script"),
        Node("F", "Formatter: targets, cover letter, AI disclosure, checklist"),
        Node("WG", WRITING, "maybe"),
    )
    edges = (
        Edge("I", "S"),
        *search_edges,
        Edge("A", "OA"),
        Edge("OA", "A", "restructuring, no cap", "back"),
        Edge("OA", "B"),
        Edge("B", "W"),
        Edge("B", "V"),
        Edge("V", "V", "checks the rendered figure, up to 2 fixes, optional", "back"),
        Edge("W", "C"),
        Edge("W", "AB"),
        Edge("C", "SC", "a claim not held, after every claim is judged"),
        Edge("C", "P"),
        Edge("AB", "P"),
        Edge("V", "P"),
        Edge("P", "WR", "a revision: round 2, once", "dotted"),
        Edge("WR", "IC"),
        Edge("IC", "P", "", "back"),
        Edge("P", "SD", "after round 2 a score under 6.5 with a Critical issue open"),
        Edge("P", "CF"),
        Edge("CF", "F"),
        Edge("F", "F", "a checklist item fails, no cap", "back"),
        Edge("F", "WG"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, edges)


def paper_revision_graph() -> Graph:
    nodes = (
        Node("P", "Peer reviewer: the comments as a roadmap"),
        Node("W", "Draft writer: a patch per item"),
        Node("SD", "You decide", "stop"),
        Node("C", "Citation compliance: each claim against its source's full text"),
        Node("SC", "A claim the run cannot support", "stop"),
        Node("R2", "Peer reviewer: the re-review"),
        Node("W2", "Draft writer: the items not resolved", "maybe"),
        Node("CF", PATCHED, "script"),
        Node("RL", "Draft writer: the response letter, for referee comments", "maybe"),
        Node("RC", "Peer reviewer: the letter checked, a verdict per point", "maybe"),
        Node("WG", WRITING, "maybe"),
        Node("X", "Revised draft, the rest as limitations"),
    )
    edges = (
        Edge("P", "W"),
        Edge("W", "SD", "a structural patch, or a patch rejected twice"),
        Edge("W", "C"),
        Edge("C", "SC", "a claim not held"),
        Edge("C", "R2", "round 2, once"),
        Edge("R2", "W2"),
        Edge("W2", "CF"),
        Edge("R2", "CF"),
        Edge("CF", "RL"),
        Edge("RL", "RC"),
        Edge("RC", "WG"),
        Edge("CF", "WG", "a /writing report as the comments"),
        Edge("WG", "X"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, edges)


def paper_outline_only_graph() -> Graph:
    search_nodes, search_edges = _search("A")
    nodes = (
        Node("I", "Intake agent: you confirm", "stop"),
        *search_nodes,

    nodes = (

            "SS",

        Edge("C", "SD", "a claim not in its source, or a reference no lookup confirms"),
        Edge("C", "X"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, edges)


def paper_disclosure_graph() -> Graph:
    nodes = (
        Node(
            "S",
            "You name the venue, and paste the policy of a venue it does not know",
            "stop",
        ),
        Node("F", "Formatter: each kind of AI use marked, the statement as a patch"),
        Node("U", "You confirm each uncertain kind of AI use", "stop"),
    )
    edges = (
        Edge("S", "F"),
        Edge("F", "U", "a kind marked uncertain"),
        Edge("U", "F", "your answer", "back"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, edges)


def paper_plan_graph() -> Graph:
    nodes = (
        Node("I", "Intake agent: 3 questions, you confirm", "stop"),
        Node("M", "Socratic mentor, chapter by chapter, given each answer"),
        Node("Q", "The mentor's question", "stop"),
        Node("S", "Structure architect: the outline"),
        Node("M2", "Socratic mentor and argument builder: the stress test"),
        Node("X", "Chapter plan and INSIGHT collection"),
    )
    edges = (
        Edge("I", "M"),
        Edge("M", "Q"),
        Edge("Q", "M", "offers after 8 rounds on a chapter, 30 in all", "back"),
        Edge("M", "S"),
        Edge("S", "M2"),
        Edge("M2", "X"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, edges)


def paper_format_convert_graph() -> Graph:
    nodes = (
        Node("F", "Formatter: LaTeX, DOCX, PDF, Markdown, citation style, as patches"),
        Node(
            "F2",
            "Formatter, resumed: the conversion patch, when the citation style changes",

        ),

    )

def paper_revision_coach_graph() -> Graph:
    nodes = (
        Node("R", "Revision coach: the comments parsed"),
        Node("Q", "You confirm the parsed comments", "stop"),
        Node("R2", "Revision coach, resumed: roadmap, tracking template, letter skeleton"),
    )

        Edge("R", "Q"),

    )
    return _graph(nodes, edges)


def paper_rebuttal_audit_graph() -> Graph:
    nodes = (
        Node("R", "Revision coach, parsing only: the comments and your response draft"),
        Node("X", "Coverage per comment, gaps, risk flags; advisory, nothing verified"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, (Edge("R", "X"),))


def paper_response_letter_graph() -> Graph:
    nodes = (
        Node("W", "Draft writer: response-letter.md beside the manuscript"),
        Node("P", "Peer reviewer, fresh: a verdict per point"),
        Node("X", "The letter and its check"),
    )
    return _graph(nodes, (Edge("W", "P"), Edge("P", "X")))


PAPER_MODES = (
    ("full", paper_full_graph),
    ("revision", paper_revision_graph),
    ("lit-review", paper_lit_review_graph),
    ("outline-only", paper_outline_only_graph),
    ("abstract-only", paper_abstract_only_graph),
    ("citation-check", paper_citation_check_graph),
    ("disclosure", paper_disclosure_graph),
    ("plan", paper_plan_graph),
    ("format-convert", paper_format_convert_graph),
    ("revision-coach", paper_revision_coach_graph),
    ("rebuttal-audit", paper_rebuttal_audit_graph),
    ("response-letter", paper_response_letter_graph),
)

GOAL = "[5.Q (i)]"

        "stop",
    ),
)

)

)

    nodes = (
        N("Q", "Session asks: the targets, the format profile, the materials", "stop"),
        N(
            "PD",
            f"Script, run by the session: pandoc, a .md or a .docx to the .tex every "
            f"target is converted from {GOAL}",
            "script",
        ),
        N(
            "F",
            f"Phase 7 Formatter: citation style, venue template, format profile, as "
            f"patches; the report {GOAL}",
            "ruled",
        ),
        N(
            "F2",
            f"Formatter, resumed: the conversion patch, when the citation style changes "
            f"{GOAL}",
            "ruled_maybe",
        ),
        N(
            "CV",
            f"Script, run by the session: the patches applied, latexmk for the PDF, pandoc "
            f"for DOCX and Markdown {GOAL}",
            "script",
        ),
        N(
            "K",
            f"Script, run by the session: the headings, figures, tables and abstract "
            f"counted in each output {GOAL}",
            "script",
        ),
        N("X", "Formatted document"),
    )
    edges = (
        E("Q", "PD", "a .md or a .docx"),
        E("Q", "F"),
        E("PD", "F"),
        E("F", "F2"),
        E("F", "CV"),
        E("F2", "CV"),
        E("CV", "K"),
        E("K", "X"),
    )
    return _g(nodes, edges, PATCH_STOPS, WIDE_WIDTH)


def ap_revision_coach_simple() -> gf.Graph:
    nodes = (
        N("R", "Revision coach: the referee comments"),
        N("X", "Revision roadmap, tracking template, response letter skeleton"),
    )
    return _g(nodes, (E("R", "X"),))


def ap_rebuttal_audit_simple() -> gf.Graph:
    nodes = (
        N("R", "Revision coach, parsing only: the comments and your response draft"),
        N("X", "Coverage per comment, gaps, risk flags"),
    )
    return _g(nodes, (E("R", "X"),))


def ap_response_letter_simple() -> gf.Graph:
    nodes = (
        N("W", "Step 3: the response letter, each point with its change and place"),
        N("C", "Step 4: the re-review, a verdict per point"),
    )
    return _g(nodes, (E("W", "C"),))


def ap_revision_coach_detailed() -> gf.Graph:
    nodes = (
        N("RC", "Revision coach: parse, classify, map, prioritize every comment"),
        N("RV", "You validate the parsed comments", "stop"),
        N("RM", "Revision roadmap"),
        N("RT", "Tracking template and response letter skeleton, when you ask"),
        N("RL", "Schema 11 commitment ledger, in the pipeline"),
    )
    edges = (
        E("RC", "RV"),
        E("RV", "RC", "a correction", "back"),
        E("RV", "RM"),
        E("RM", "RT"),
        E("RM", "RL"),
    )
    return _g(nodes, edges, width=WIDE_WIDTH)


def paper_revision_coach_detailed() -> gf.Graph:
    nodes = (
        N("RC", "Revision coach: parse, classify, map, prioritize every comment"),
        N("RV", "You validate the parsed comments", "stop"),
        N("RM", "Revision coach, resumed: revision roadmap"),
        N("RT", "Tracking template and response letter skeleton, when you ask"),
        N(
            "RL",
            f"commitments.md in the run folder, in place of the Schema 11 ledger {LETTER}",
            "ruled",
        ),
    )
    edges = (
        E("RC", "RV"),
        E("RV", "RC", "a correction", "back"),
        E("RV", "RM"),
        E("RM", "RT"),
        E("RM", "RL"),
    )
    return _g(nodes, edges, width=WIDE_WIDTH)


def ap_rebuttal_audit_detailed() -> gf.Graph:
    nodes = (
        N("G", "both the comments and a response draft given?", "choice"),
        N("RC", "Route to revision-coach"),
        N("RA", "Revision coach, parsing only"),
        N("X", "Coverage table, gap list, risk flags, suggestions; no Schema 11, no verified status"),
    )
    edges = (E("G", "RC", "the comments only"), E("G", "RA", "both"), E("RA", "X"))
    return _g(nodes, edges, width=WIDE_WIDTH)


def paper_rebuttal_audit_detailed() -> gf.Graph:
    nodes = (
        N("G", "both the comments and a response draft given?", "choice"),
        N("RC", "Route to revision-coach"),
        N("RA", "Revision coach, parsing only"),
        N(
            "X",
            f"rebuttal-audit.md: coverage table, gap list, risk flags, suggestions; no "
            f"commitments.md, nothing marked verified {LETTER}",
            "ruled",
        ),
    )
    edges = (E("G", "RC", "the comments only"), E("G", "RA", "both"), E("RA", "X"))
    return _g(nodes, edges, width=WIDE_WIDTH)


def ap_response_letter_detailed() -> gf.Graph:
    nodes = (
        N("W", "Step 3: the author writes the response letter, point by point"),
        N(
            "C",
            "Step 4: re-review, completeness, consistency, truthfulness, a verdict per point",
        ),
        N("X", "Verdicts; no reassessment of the paper, no new requests"),
    )
    return _g(nodes, (E("W", "C"), E("C", "X")), width=WIDE_WIDTH)


def paper_response_letter_detailed() -> gf.Graph:
    nodes = (
        N(
            "W",
            f"Draft writer, fresh: response-letter.md beside the manuscript, in the response "
            f"template's form {LETTER}",
            "ruled",
        ),
        N(
            "C",
            f"Peer reviewer, fresh: completeness, consistency, truthfulness, a verdict per "
            f"point, response-letter-check.md {LETTER}",
            "ruled",
        ),
        N("X", "Verdicts in the final message; no manuscript file changed"),
    )
    return _g(nodes, (E("W", "C"), E("C", "X")), width=WIDE_WIDTH)

### Departures from item 6 of the rules

The rules of `docs/dev/skill-layout.md:54-57` ask one rule per bullet and a cell that holds a phrase or a sentence. Two places keep a longer form, each because the text is one rule that the reader needs whole:
- The Stops table's cells (`SKILL.md:892` to `:911`) hold the moment, the content and the answer of each stop, some in two or three sentences, since a stop's moment and its content are one rule split over two columns and a cell cut short would lose when the stop is raised.
- The "Stops this mode can reach" line of each mode is one sentence that lists the stops of that mode, since it is a list, not a set of rules.

### Judgments the round left open

- "The mode to run" is drawn on every paper figure, since `SKILL.md`, Steps 1, raises it for any mode before intake.
- `slr_lineage`, restored in the anchor protocol's inputs (item 1), has no setter in `paper`; the protocol's cold-start `mode=` input sets it, as the source says, and no sentence of `paper` claims an orchestrator that sets it.
- Where items are left out, the source's numbers of the items kept are kept (credit-statement-template Step 5, abstract-bilingual items 4 and 5 and Step 4, failure-paths F2 to F5 and the handling steps 1, 2 and 5, the citation-compliance table rows), so a page still matches its source item by item.
- `references/intake.md`'s heading "Interview Order" names the section left after the handoff block is removed.
- `provenance_summary.md` stays the formatter's output for a version family, as academic-paper's formatter writes it.
